- Ownership: coordinator alone edits product, docs, scripts and commits. Acquisition agent owns `scratch/acquisition/`; analysis agent owns `scratch/old-analysis/`; GPT-6.1 internal reviewer owns `scratch/reviewer/`. Agents hand proposed patches back for coordinator application. Coordinator also drafts Step 0 because the available agent slots include the coordinator.
- Isolation exceptions are precisely the user-mandated new worktree Git registration, new branch, shared `~/.worldline-heavy.lock`, and transient systemd scopes. All task artifacts, temporary files and caches remain inside this worktree. No other checkout, branch, Cosmogony file, installed runtime or process is modified. No external vault pin, SIA note, or publication is written under this run's stricter isolation instruction.
- Reversal: stop only owned commands; retain evidence; do not remove this or any other worktree during the run. Revert this branch's later product commits individually if needed.

## D1 — heavy-job admission and containment

- Status: APPROVE product heavy by actual GPT-6.1 after contained negative self-tests; launcher SHA256 c3c7fcc84bb509668d0136606449486d1179d67d7c89bf68f6f5db79a4ece61a.
- Options: uncontained command; raise historical cap; bounded scope at unchanged cap. Choice: `scripts/phase1_heavy.py` launches `flock /home/sicarii/.worldline-heavy.lock` and checks `/proc/meminfo` only inside the lock. Require at least `14GiB` (conservative interpretation of requested `14GB`); otherwise release and retry after five minutes, recording each check.
- Every launched job has `systemd-run --user --scope`, `MemoryMax=8G`, `MemorySwapMax=0`, explicit wall deadline, RUSAGE_CHILDREN process peak RSS (GNU time unavailable), streamed stdout/stderr and cgroup samples. Solver invocations additionally require `--memlimit` and `--timeout`; level and full scope remain unchanged. Historical acquisition launcher explicitly used MemorySwapMax=0; acquisition copies and hashes identify that original setting in scratch/acquisition/historical-index-v1.json. The option remains explicit for faithful replay.
- Evidence: initial review rejected exit-code retry ambiguity and insufficient descendant cleanup; retained at scratch/reviewer/step0-review-draft.txt. Revised launcher requires an admission marker, independent supervisor samples, actual cap validation and positive terminal unit state before unlocking. Contained negative self-tests passed as recorded in records/phase1-blockers/launcher-verification.json; reviewer approved product heavy, old minimal probes and acquisition stage measurements. The original full-build baseline follows the unchanged scope under this authorization; no product guarantee is inferred from launcher tests. Launcher output is measurement, never proof of product correctness. A killed recorder can leave the final cgroup sample absent; absence stays UNKNOWN and requires external scope/journal corroboration.
- Reversal: cease using launcher, retain all logs; replace it only with a reviewer-approved launcher preserving or strengthening the same admission, scope and retention rules. Never raise the cap implicitly.

No sustained MemoryHigh throttling is established by the retained high-count observations. Parent hierarchical events include descendant work and do not independently identify parent-local reclaim. ## D115 — Wake the runtime observer on the intent write

The selected qualification carrier remains `scratch/old-analysis/full_runtime_supervisor_v3.py`. Its preimage is `3105162b3f9eac3a61460e9a1cb23966a84296fa9e4af9ddc69c1155e97b39e1`. The successor is `8c2c222015627f3dbb0a0a2529dc0c6570f7ef3d9e8efdc6511729b739651255`.

The 50 ms idle poll could notice a fsynced `popen.jsonl` intent only after `--collect` had removed a unit that exited in a few milliseconds. The sampler now waits on `inotifywait` for create, close_write, and moved_to under the attempt directory, then uses the existing 1 ms / 0.25 s typed-manager retry. If `inotifywait` exits, the loop falls back to a 1 ms wait. The ExecStart equality predicate, unit grammar, ownership rules, and qualification roster are unchanged.

A local probe wrote the intent, launched `systemd-run --user --collect --unit wl-phase1-observerwake3.service /usr/bin/true`, and matched typed ExecStart `["/usr/bin/true"]` on the second query. That is a timing probe, not a qualification result. The original qualification has not been re-run. Phase 1 is not accepted. Independent acceptance remains NOT PERFORMED.


## D116 — Repair observed semantic regressions before final qualification

**Options:** retain proof-driven helpers despite changed behavior, add input caps
or empty-result fallbacks, disable checked equations, or repair implementations
with the original domains/contracts and failure evidence intact. Choose the last.
No proof floor, original gate, assumption policy or deployment boundary changes.

**Evidence and choices:**

- The unchanged SHA vector caught lost incoming-state feed-forward: restore all
  eight additions. Remove redundant self-Posts only where identical complete
  transparent definitions remain; retain production compression and independent
  append/replay contracts. Cache repeated reservation-model terms instead of
  repeatedly evaluating them in added assertions. Evidence: semantic controls,
  v3/v4 and v2 crypto/authority reviews.
- A direct valid 16 MiB decoder call raised `Storage_Error`: construct its exact
  result in place with one count/write decoder. Replace renderer prefix replay
  with advancing cursors and decoded-key indexing, preserving first occurrence
  order/last value. Share escaped-size/direct-output operations instead of full
  quoted temporaries in count passes. The limited scratch index does not replace
  O1's original nonlimited independent owning values. Evidence: decoder
  discriminator, v5/v8 and the 519-case native oracle.
- The ordinary policy-positive exposed a false empty declaration: require zero
  canonical required entries and retain explicit-empty/absent distinction.
  Oversized supplied recovery operands fail explicitly, never select absence.
  Actual-bound offsets replace whole-input rebasing copies. Evidence: v3
  failure, v4's 88 controls and v8 native smoke.
- Three source-screen counterexamples compile with the actual GNAT profile:
  share one Ada tokenizer for screening/pins, decode Latin-1/BOM-selected UTF-8,
  recognize RM identifier categories/Unicode separators and preserve literal
  whitespace. Derive every affected literal pin with `refresh_contract_pins.py`;
  keep the historical stale manifest refused. Evidence: v2 Unicode before/
  compiler records and 22 passing successor behavior controls.
- The real C constructor supplies 26 instructions/208 bytes; the old Ada/Python
  projection had 23/184 and refused it. Match the actual user-namespace ENOSPC
  branch, not a weakened constructor or substituted expected observation.
  Evidence: actual producer/native-consumer before refusal and v8 acceptance.
  This component smoke does not establish independent kernel-program/custody
  observation.

**Review:** four actual-model v2 internal source reviews reported REQUEST CHANGES.
Successor repairs need fresh exact-source review and full proof/runtime
qualification. The owned pre-freeze proof was cancelled for those repairs,
not converted into a pass.

**Reversal:** a separate reviewed source revert if disproved; retain failed runs,
snapshots, controls and original requirements. Never suppress checks, hand-pin
digests, return successful empty data or relabel finite controls as phase exit.

the paired permitted inline, harmless alias and dotless-identifier witnesses.

## D120 — Make supervisor poll state local and explicit

**Options:** retain mutable shared poll outputs overwritten by later calls;
return each poll's complete state as a local limited value; or silence all
unused-output warnings.

**Choice:** use a fresh state record. Initial reads consume readability and
break facts; final replay consumes its own started/failure/stop/first-exit
facts. Every original validation, occurrence/ordering requirement and retained
event comparison remains. Exit ordinal is meaningful only when an exit was
seen; it is now local to that same poll. Removed initializers were overwritten
before reads, as checked by flow analysis. Source_Link retains the same
short-circuit predicates; the initial invalid-input return directly assigns
its reason without updating a join variable that cannot be consumed.

The only deliberately unused outputs are zero-element arrays from count-only
scans. An explicit post-call `pragma Unused` documents this fact, following the
installed compiler reference manual. No assertion, proof check, accepted case,
coverage requirement or proof floor is disabled.

`--warnings=error` and no warning/error diagnostics. The first trial with

source pin correctly rejected the changed body; the existing deterministic

## D122 — Express startup scanner progress without changing its accepted grammar

**Problem:** aggregate flow could not establish termination of envelope scans,
and contextual inlining exposed unused outputs/overwritten initializers.

**Decision:** require Next_Line to preserve cursor monotonicity and advance on
a valid line; add bounded field/cursor invariants and loop variants. Use dedicated
local discarded-header values instead of declaring a subsequently consumed line
unused. Make Role_Selected's already-required valid-line check explicit. Remove
only initializers overwritten before every read, including the fully populated
HMAC pads. Bind Policy_Syntax.Probe's computed result extent to a constant after
a separately observed E0007. Preserve schemas, full values, assertions and gates.

**Verification:** focused strict level-3 Next_Line proof passes 35 reported checks,
zero unproved/justified and no Assume; the initial two-index-check failure is
retained. Aggregate flow remains FAILED on namespace-projection findings. Checked
build, original Ada tests/fuzz and the selected 22 Python tests pass. The Python
roster includes twelve native scanner and six glob-policy subcases. No complete
proof or independent acceptance is claimed.

**Risk:** scanner contracts must hold on malformed and boundary inputs as well as
valid lines. Fixed ordinary native cases exercise both; they cannot establish
protected source authenticity. The added Valid branch repeats a fact required
by the preceding complete Envelope_Valid scan, rather than narrowing the accepted
language. Full source/host/retirement/actor acceptance remains unresolved.

**Reversal:** a new corrective commit if evidence contradicts the change; preserve
the counterexample, all prior runs and the original acceptance requirements.

raw-policy test's expected `empty=True` for required `['a', 'z']`. D116 explicitly
requires zero canonical required entries, and the production body is byte-identical
to base b560c69. Preserve the original test file, failed run, hashes and rationale
under `records/phase1-direct/policy-empty-oracle-correction`; change only this
stale expected boolean to False. All fixture inputs, roster equality, bundle,
explicit-empty and absent controls remain. This strengthens detection of a false
empty declaration; no production behavior or acceptance requirement is weakened.
The subsequent 22-test run passes at unchanged native source/library identities.


## D123 — Bound namespace scans by offsets and expose PID progress

**Problem:** the namespace map scanner advanced an absolute array index past
E.Count'Last. A one-byte rebased array demonstrates Constraint_Error in the exact
old helper. Aggregate flow also could not establish namespace scan termination.

**Decision:** scan map lines with offsets in 0 .. Raw'Length and convert to an
array index only for an actual byte. Preserve newline, interval, overlap and
uint32 validation rules. Stop a malformed unmatched final map line at the
existing overflow sentinel. Explicitly consume the first nonspace PID byte
before scanning the rest of that word, retaining the same delimiter grammar and
decimal rejection. State bounded cursor invariants and progress variants.
Private helper declarations allow a child test to exercise production bodies;
no public authorization interface or SPARK-Off boundary is added.

**Evidence:** focused strict level-3 proofs pass Map_Line_End (14 checks),
Map_Valid (44), Map_Value (17), and Last_PID (24). Earlier PID proof failed its
outer-loop variant and is retained. Reports share a compiler cache; unrelated
entries and excluded functions with zero checks are not credited as new proofs.
Map proofs precede the PID-only rewrite, with their bodies unchanged.
The checked canonical build, original Ada tests/fuzz and namespace child controls
pass at the final source/library hashes. The exact old helper reproduces
Constraint_Error; its first harness compile failed a missing operator visibility
clause, now corrected with failure evidence retained. The v3 runner reuses the
passed v2 build only after verifying unchanged source and library hashes.
See records/phase1-direct/namespace-verification-summary.json.

**Risk and limits:** ordinary byte fixtures do not authenticate namespaces,
credentials or protected custody. Aggregate flow is still FAILED on the next
resource-ledger exception-handler diagnostics. Full-domain retirement, whole
strict proof, original complete runtime, protected/actor execution and independent
F7 acceptance remain open. No contract, proof floor or accepted case is waived.

**Reversal:** a new corrective commit with the original failure evidence retained.


## D124 — Keep typed refusal cleanup reachable and canonicalize ledger output bytes

**Problem:** strict flow rejected the typed resource-ledger exception handlers as
unreachable. Subsequent native controls exposed unspecified padding copied into
Quantity_C/Result_C outputs, breaking deterministic byte correspondence.

**Decision:** declare a local Malformed_Input exception in Compute and Headroom.
Their existing malformed-input branches now raise to the existing local handler,
which still zeros the same complete outputs, supplies Invalid_Input and returns
255. No exception propagates from those explicit branches. Normal computation,
validation predicates, snapshots and all unexpected-exception cleanup remain.
Remove the overwritten Typed_Rows initializer; retain Slots initialization as
required by verified flow. The public typed spec and callers remain unchanged.

The existing C-boundary Write_Quantity and Write_Result now initialize each full
owned output extent and assign individual fields, instead of copying unspecified
Ada record padding. All prior extent/alignment/alias checks and transport behavior
remain; no authorization arithmetic moves out of SPARK.

**Rejected alternative:** moving cleanup solely to the foreign boundary passed
focused proof but was rejected after the alias-aware inventory found the direct
Custody_V2_Machine_Evidence.Joined caller. Both the candidate patch and its result
are retained. This demonstrates why a local proof pass was not acceptance.

**Evidence:** strict level-3 proof passes the typed module with zero reported
warnings/errors/unproved/justified and no pragma Assume. Selected Compute and
Headroom report 17 and 7 checks respectively; helpers have their own retained
entries. Unrelated shared-cache report entries receive no new proof credit.
The checked canonical build, original Ada behavior/fuzz, actual typed malformed
cleanup controls and 40 selected Python resource tests pass with source/library
hash equality before/after. Earlier compile, flow and runtime failures remain
under records/phase1-direct/ledger-*.

The native ownership test had also expected a legacy lifecycle call from a
production facade that already uses the ordinary full-protocol API. Preserve
all old span checks and add an explicit legacy call; additionally observe the
actual ordinary API, its input/output extents and result. No old ABI or assertion
is dropped. New padding assertions require full bytes to match zero-initialized
ctypes records; they do not hide the defect by comparing only semantic fields.

**Risk and limits:** locally handled malformed inputs now use the Ada exception
mechanism; the same routine catches them before returning, without changing the
public contract or allowing nonlocal propagation. Compiler/runtime exception
machinery and the existing foreign writes remain in the trusted runtime boundary.
Finite controls do not prove arbitrary host allocation/stack or authenticated
custody. Full-source strict proof, original full runtime, retirement, protected/
actor controls and independent acceptance remain open. No proof floor is lowered.

**Reversal:** new corrective commit; preserve all original negative evidence.


**D124 pin review:** the existing typed-export guard rejected the changed C
transport source as intended. One separately invoked actual GPT-6.1 reviewer
read the complete C_API body/spec, verified that only the two ledger writer
functions differ, and returned CONCUR for exact-source repin eligibility.
The receipt is ledger-transport-review.json; the source identity is derived
from its reviewed bytes, not manually supplied hash literals. The guarded
repin script rejects missing/incomplete review and changed-source controls;
normal and raw transport identities are checked after regeneration. This is
source-only review, not F7. No test/proof run was falsely attributed to the
reviewer; it inspected and hash-verified the retained runs.

The reviewer retained preexisting custody/worker exports without Native_Call_Guard
and potentially raising diagnostic calls inside custody exception handlers.
These remain separate whole-system acceptance blockers with exact anchors in
the receipt. Compiler warnings elsewhere remain. Aggregate flow clears the
ledger findings but still fails on Roots mode and discarded fold outputs.

D124 final guard validation: all 16 original typed-export correspondence tests
pass after reviewed pin derivation, including the deliberate copy, ABI, alias,
exception-return and source-literal mutation controls. This is source-guard
TESTED evidence, not compiled-mutant or whole-system proof.

## D125 — Explicit custody scratch lifetimes and policy refusal transport

**Problem:** aggregate strict flow stopped on intentionally unchanged roots,
discarded fold outputs, incomplete initialization analysis in mutation rendering,
and an unreachable policy transport exception handler. Wider runtime regression
also exposed prepared-wire internal refusals escaping its public error boundary.

**Change:** preserve the request-abandon in-out interface and unchanged-roots
postcondition; `Unmodified` declares its deliberate lack of assignment and adds
the reciprocal warning if an assignment is introduced. Post-call `Unused`
pragmas identify scratch outputs that each wrapper already discarded. These
pragmas suppress the specific unused-value diagnostics; they do not establish
proof or remove the calls, their checks, or their meaningful result consumers.
Scratch proof/length outputs that were later overwritten now have separate local
lifetimes. Remove only proven-overwritten initializers, preserving defaults that
are used on partial paths. All four touched public Ada specs remain byte-identical.

Digest rendering assigns one character per statically bounded array iteration.
The successor arena is initialized before prefix copying; decimal rendering
initializes its bounded buffer before filling the returned suffix. Existing
overflow/refusal checks, counter domains, output sizing and successor checks
remain. Malformed policy wire shapes enter the original local exception handler,
which returns the original transport code with the already-zeroed result.
No error escapes that routine, and no authorization moves out of SPARK.

Prepared-wire encoding now wraps the two specific archive/guard refusal classes
in its existing `WorldlineError` boundary, retaining the original exception as
the cause. Original tests/assertions are preserved; additional controls check
cause retention. This does not intercept protected acquisition outside the
value encoder or catch arbitrary runtime exceptions.

**Evidence and limits:** `custody-flow-source-audit.json` verifies unchanged
specs and original test methods. `custody-discard-proof-v1` passes strict level-3
proof for the request-abandon and policy-transport units. Only those named units
receive proof credit; unrelated shared-cache entries are not fresh evidence.
The checked build, original Ada behavior/fuzz and new typed policy refusal
controls pass. The first wider runtime run failed on prepared-wire exception
types; that failure is retained. Runtime v2 then exposed the native exception
escape described in D126. The final focused regression result is recorded in
`custody-discard-runtime-v3/result.json` and the verification summary.

Aggregate flow advances beyond the repaired units but still fails on startup
and recovery termination/statement diagnostics. The first mutation helper proof
times out in dependency processing without a completed report: UNKNOWN. Its
region-expanded command repeats a unit name; this observation is not a proven
tool defect. A subsequent single-subprogram digest proof completed: its eight
checks pass, with no flow errors, warnings or Assume. The shared report total
also contains unrelated cached units and is not fresh coverage. Full source
proof, original complete runtime, retirement, authentic
protected/actor evidence and independent acceptance remain open. This is a
non-final checkpoint, with no claim of Phase One completion.

## D126 — Keep custody and worker exception handlers inside the native guard

**Reproduced failure:** the real successor ABI killed its Python caller with
`ADA.IO_EXCEPTIONS.DEVICE_ERROR` while reporting an earlier exception. Thirteen
custody/worker exports lacked the native guard already used by adjacent exports;
eight of their handlers called the diagnostic emitter directly. The emitter
requires guard ownership and may itself fail on real descriptor errors.

**Repair:** extend the existing guard's lifetime over each complete exported
body and its inner handler. Queue original exceptions through the existing
capture routine while the guard is owned. The existing finalizer drains and
releases ownership; an outer handler preserves `Error_Internal` if guard
acquisition/finalization fails. No exported signature, numeric range, decision,
validation or authority contract changes. Complete-unit source review checked
the changed bodies and exact equality of unrelated C_API bytes against the
preceding checkpoint. The raw/normalized identities are derived only after that
exact-source review. Review is not independent runtime execution or F7.

**Tests:** real-library controls exercise all thirteen exports with nested
entry, a contending thread and guard release. A child process closes stderr,
provokes the existing underlying failure and verifies primary transport,
sticky diagnostic failure and a subsequent native call. The test observes
hidden C guard functions by their local ELF offsets, anchored with `dladdr`
to the same unstripped library; it neither adds a production export nor loads
a second mutex. The initial test instrumentation incorrectly expected hidden
helpers in the dynamic symbol table; that failed attempt is retained separately.

**Still FAILED:** original successor semantics require arbitrary zero padding.
The new JACKAL-exact-oracle test retains 4096-byte padding, buffer sizing,
canaries and unchanged inputs. After containment was repaired, fifteen subcases
returned `Error_Internal`: installed GNAT `System.Generic_Bignums.Normalize`
raises `Storage_Error` at its fixed `Bignum_Limit`. Checked full-value ghost
calculations reach that limit even for zero-padded small operands. Exception
containment does not satisfy the mathematical result contract. No input cap,
disabled assertion/ghost code, weaker expectation or installed runtime patch
has been substituted. This is an open full-domain numerical/runtime obligation.


## D127 — Prove structural progress through archive child contracts

Strict aggregate flow next stopped on startup/recovery loops, output-array
initialization and specialized runner branches. Add measures to the existing
terminating loops and preserve their input domains. Iterate byte/hex output by
the result index so initialization is direct. Express unchanged lazy runner
branches as conditional expressions, removing only overwritten initializers;
retain separate stage calls and actual foreign getter/equality observations.

The first strict Semantic_Same proof did not discharge recursive progress or
the dictionary match counter. Increasing a timeout would not supply the missing
interface facts. Strengthen Operation_Archive.Field with its existing input-span
length bound, expose the private Children bound, and strengthen Element with
its strict-child-or-empty result. Add Found < K at the dictionary inner loop.
These are implementation obligations, not caller assumptions or new preconditions.
No rejection branch, numeric ceiling or weakened contract is introduced.

Field's new postcondition and eight selected checks pass. Children's local
postcondition passes. A region-limited helper run selected zero checks for the
public Count/Element posts in the specification; this is explicitly not evidence
for those posts. Separate declaration-scoped proof runs verify them. The caller
now discharges eight selected checks including recursive variants and counter
overflow. Earlier failures, region coverage limits and representation-phase
timeout are retained; shared-cache totals are not fresh coverage.

All-source flow still reports scoped-capture initialization/progress diagnostics.
Only actual bounded helper/caller proof and recorded regression results establish
this checkpoint's scope. Full original proof/runtime, complete value and Old
retirement, protected/actor evidence and independent F7 remain required.

Checked build and original Ada behavior/fuzz pass. The selected Python run
passed 59 cases and hit one pre-existing ctypes fixture error before Ada was
called. The query/output union now uses correctly typed views of the identical
owned allocation. Both original refusal and unchanged-memory assertions remain;
a detached-output positive control is added. That targeted control passes
against the identical library and unchanged product sources. The initial
60-case run remains FAILED, not relabeled as a successful single suite.
`recovery-overlap-fixture-v1/reuse-and-justification.json` binds the continuation
and verifies that all other methods in that test module are unchanged.
